    sum = sum + i

// For loop with multiple increments using comma

    sum = sum + i
    if (i >= 6)
        break
assert(sum == 12)

// Nested for loops
total = 0
for (i = 0; i < 2; i = i + 1)
    for (j = 0; j < 2; j = j + 1)
        total = total + (i * 2 + j)
assert(total == 6)

[0;32m[ RUN      ] [mTestLangCommon.ForLoopSemicolonsSyntax
Script:
// For loop with standard semicolon syntax
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 5; i = i + 1)
    sum = sum + i
assert(sum == 10)

// For loop with multiple initializations using comma
product = 0
for (i = 1, j = 10; i <= 3; i = i + 1)
    product = i * j
assert(product == 30)

// For loop with multiple increments using comma
sum = 0
for (i = 0, j = 5; i < 3; i = i + 1, j = j - 1)
    sum = sum + i + j
assert(sum == 15)

// For loop with empty sections but semicolons present
count = 0
i = 0
for (; i < 3; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty condition section
count = 0
for (i = 0; ; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty increment section
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 3; )
    sum = sum + i
    i = i + 1
assert(sum == 3)

// For loop with all sections empty
count = 0
for (;;)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with complex expressions
result = 0
add_one = fun(x) { return x + 1 }
for (i = add_one(0); i < add_one(2); i = add_one(i))
    result = result + i
assert(result == 3)

// Test simple variable assignment and comparison
'w 'z # 
'w 'y #
z y == assert

// Test non-existent variable
'foo exists not assert

// Test variable assignment and retrieval
42 'a #
'a exists assert
a 42 == assert

// Test variable arithmetic
2 'b #
a b + 44 == assert

// p is a 'pointer' to object called 'a
'a 'p #
'a p == assert // test Label/Pathname equivalence

// 'dereference' the 'pointer'
p @ 42 == assert

// can access outer scope
{ a } & 42 == assert
{ p @ } & 42 == assert

// Test non-existent variable
'e exists not assert

// name resolution works up the context stack
{ 5 'e # { 'e exists assert } & } &

// e was local to the previous continuation
'e exists not assert

// Test continuation as function
{ + } 'plus #
1 2 + 3 == assert
1 2 plus & 3 == assert

// Complex nested continuation
{ 7 'e # { e 2 } & { + } 'p # p & } & 9 == assert

// Test variable scope in continuation
{ 1 'qq # 'qq exists assert } & 'qq exists not assert

// Ensure only one value left on stack (our initial 1)
depth 1 == assert

// Clean up at the end
clear

Script:
// For loop with standard semicolon syntax
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 5; i = i + 1)
    sum = sum + i
assert(sum == 10)

// For loop with multiple initializations using comma
product = 0
for (i = 1, j = 10; i <= 3; i = i + 1)
    product = i * j
assert(product == 30)

// For loop with multiple increments using comma
sum = 0
for (i = 0, j = 5; i < 3; i = i + 1, j = j - 1)
    sum = sum + i + j
assert(sum == 15)

// For loop with empty sections but semicolons present
count = 0
i = 0
for (; i < 3; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty condition section
count = 0
for (i = 0; ; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty increment section
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 3; )
    sum = sum + i
    i = i + 1
assert(sum == 3)

// For loop with all sections empty
count = 0
for (;;)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with complex expressions
result = 0
add_one = fun(x) { return x + 1 }
for (i = add_one(0); i < add_one(2); i = add_one(i))
    result = result + i
assert(result == 3)
